string; } | { type: "NodeCompleted"; nodeId: string; resultHash?: string; } | { type: "NodeFailed"; nodeId: string; errorCode: string; } | { type: "ContinueAsNew"; nextRunId: string; summaryHash: string; } | { type: "WorkflowCompleted"; resultHash?: string; } | { type: "WorkflowFailed"; errorCode: string; }; Large inputs/results should be stored in injected blob provider. Events contain hashes and references rather than unbounded payloads. 4. Atomic registry 4.1 Registration types interface CapabilityRequirement { resource: string; // e. g. "net: api. example. com" operations: string[]; // e. g. ["GET"] constraints?: Record; } interface RetryPolicy { maxAttempts: number; backoffMs: number; maxBackoffMs: number; retryableErrors: string[]; deterministicJitter?: boolean; } interface SandboxProfile { kind: "in-process" | "worker" | "command" | "microvm"; allowNet?: string[]; allowRead?: string[]; allowWrite?: string[]; env?: Record; memoryMb?: number; cpuMs?: number; timeoutMs: number; secrets?: string[]; } interface ActionDependencies { crypto: CryptoPort; network: NetworkPort; memory: MemoryPort; telemetry: TelemetryPort; } interface AtomicAction { ( input: I, context: AtomicExecutionContext, deps: ActionDependencies, ): Promise; } interface ActionSpec { name: string; version: string; inputSchema: Json; outputSchema: Json; capabilities: CapabilityRequirement[]; sandbox: SandboxProfile; timeoutMs: number; retry: RetryPolicy; /** * If true, provider must accept idempotency key and guarantee * duplicate calls have same externally observable effect. */ requiresProviderIdempotency: boolean; execute: AtomicAction; } interface AtomicExecutionContext { tenantId: string; workflowId: string; runId: string; commandId: string; idempotencyKey: string; attempt: number; capabilityToken: string; traceParent?: string; } class AtomicRegistry { #actions = new Map(); register(spec: ActionSpec): void { validateSchema(spec.inputSchema); validateSchema(spec.outputSchema); validateSandbox(spec.sandbox); validateRetry(spec.retry); const key = `${spec.name}@${spec.version}`; if (this.#actions.has(key)) { throw new Error(`Action already registered: ${key}`); } this.#actions.set(key, spec); } resolve(name: string, version: string): ActionSpec { const spec = this.#actions.get(`${name}@${version}`); if (!spec) throw new Error(`Unknown atomic action ${name}@${version}`); return spec; } } Example custom registration: registry. register({ name: "http. getJson", version: "1.0.0", inputSchema: { type: "object", required: ["url"], properties: { url: { type: "string" } }, }, outputSchema: { type: "object", required: ["status", "body"], }, capabilities: [{ resource: "net:api.example.com", operations: ["GET"], }], sandbox: { kind: "microvm", allowNet: ["api.example.com:443"], memoryMb: 768, timeoutMs: 15_000, }, timeoutMs: 15_000, retry: { maxAttempts: 4, backoffMs: 500, maxBackoffMs: 30_000, retryableErrors: ["TIMEOUT", "HTTP_429", "HTTP_503"], }, requiresProviderIdempotency: false, async execute(input, ctx, deps) { const result = await deps.network.request( { url: input.url as string, method: "GET", headers: { "x-idempotency-key": ctx.idempotencyKey, }, }, { allowHosts: ["api.example.com"], }, ); return { status: result.status, body: JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(result.body)), }; }, }); registry must reject action whose requested sandbox allows more resources than its capability requirements. For example, action requiring net: api. example. com cannot receive unrestricted allowNet: ["*"] profile. 5. Atomic invocation and zero-trust boundary Every call authenticated and authorized independently. 5.1 Signed call envelope interface AtomicCall { tenantId: string; workflowId: string; runId: string; action: string; actionVersion: string; commandId: string; idempotencyKey: string; input: Json; inputHash: string; capabilityToken: string; issuedAt: number; expiresAt: number; nonce: string; traceParent?: string; signature: string; } signature covers canonical representation of all fields except signature: function unsignedCall(call: Omit): Uint8Array { return new TextEncoder(). encode(canonicalJson(call)); } signer may be: HMAC-SHA-256 for single trusted tenant boundary; Ed25519 for distributed agents and asymmetric verification; KMS/HSM-backed signing adapter; test signer using deterministic test keys. WebCrypto implements Ed25519/HMAC where supported. workflow does not call WebCrypto directly. 5.2 Invocation wrapper async function invokeAtomic( call: AtomicCall, registry: AtomicRegistry, state: DurableState, ports: PrimitivePorts, ): Promise { const spec = registry. resolve(call. action, call. actionVersion); await ports. permissions. verify( call.capabilityToken, { resource: `action:${spec.name}`, operations: ["execute"], constraints: { tenantId: call.tenantId, workflowId: call.workflowId, }, }, call, ); const existing = await state. get( k.actionResult(call.idempotencyKey), ); if (existing. value!== null) { await writeAudit(state, { type: "ATOMIC_REPLAY_HIT", tenantId: call.tenantId, workflowId: call.workflowId, runId: call.runId, action: call.action, commandId: call.commandId, idempotencyKey: call.idempotencyKey, resultHash: await ports.crypto.sha256( ports.crypto.canonicalJson(existing.value), ), at: ports.clock.now(), }); return existing.value; } const startedAt = ports. clock. now(); try { const output = await ports.telemetry.span( `atomic.${spec.name}`, { tenant_id: call.tenantId, workflow_id: call.workflowId, run_id: call.runId, command_id: call.commandId, action: spec.name, }, () => ports.execution.execute( spec.sandbox, () => spec.execute( call.input, { tenantId: call.tenantId, workflowId: call.workflowId, runId: call.runId, commandId: call.commandId, idempotencyKey: call.idempotencyKey, attempt: 1, capabilityToken: call.capabilityToken, traceParent: call.traceParent, }, { crypto: ports.crypto, network: ports.network, memory: ports.memory, telemetry: ports.telemetry, }, ), spec.timeoutMs, ), ) as Json; // This is a conditional write. If a duplicate worker already wrote the // result, this invocation returns the already-persisted result. const resultKey = k.actionResult(call.idempotencyKey); const oldResult = await state.get(resultKey); const commit = await state.commit((tx) => tx .check(oldResult) .set(resultKey, output), ); const result = commit.ok ? output : (await state.get(resultKey)).value!; await writeAudit(state, { type: "ATOMIC_COMPLETED", tenantId: call.tenantId, workflowId: call.workflowId, runId: call.runId, action: call.action, commandId: call.commandId, idempotencyKey: call.idempotencyKey, durationMs: ports.clock.now() - startedAt, resultHash: await ports.crypto.sha256( ports.crypto.canonicalJson(result), ), at: ports.clock.now(), }); return result; } catch (error) { await writeAudit(state, { type: "ATOMIC_FAILED", tenantId: call.tenantId, workflowId: call.workflowId, runId: call.runId, action: call.action, commandId: call.commandId, idempotencyKey: call.idempotencyKey, errorCode: classifyError(error), at: ports.clock.now(), }); throw error; } } Exactly-once limitation KV can guarantee result record written once, but cannot atomically commit with arbitrary external side effect such as sending email or charging card. Therefore: same logical attempt always uses same idempotencyKey. External providers must accept and persist key. provider must return original result for duplicate requests. If provider cannot do, action marked requiresProviderIdempotency: false and workflow must use compensating action or outbox protocol. unsafe failure window: external side effect succeeds worker crashes KV result not written worker retries No workflow engine can eliminate window without cooperation from side-effect provider or transactional resource boundary. 6. Event append with Deno KV CAS class DenoDurableState implements DurableState { constructor( private readonly kv: Deno.Kv, private readonly auditKv: Deno.Kv, ) {} get(key: KvKey): Promise> { return this.kv.get(key); } async *list(prefix: KvKey): AsyncIterable> { for await (const entry of this.kv.list({ prefix })) { yield entry; } } async commit( build: (tx: DurableTransaction) => DurableTransaction, ) { const tx = build(new DenoTransaction(this.kv)); return await tx.commit(); } async appendEvent( run: RunRef, event: WorkflowEvent, ): Promise<{ ok: true; seq: number } | { ok: false }> { const headKey = k.runHead(run.tenantId, run.workflowId, run.runId); for (;;) { const head = await this.kv.get(headKey); const previous = head.value ?? { nextSeq: 0, stateHash: "" }; const seq = previous.nextSeq; const tx = this.kv .atomic() .check(head) .set(k.event(run.tenantId, run.workflowId, run.runId, seq), event) .set(headKey, { nextSeq: seq + 1, stateHash: await hashEvent(event), }); const committed = await tx.commit(); if (committed.ok) { return { ok: true, seq }; } // Another executor appended first. Re-read and retry. } } async appendAudit(record: AuditRecord): Promise { const id = crypto.randomUUID(); await this.auditKv.set( ["audit", record.tenantId, record.at, id], record, ); } async *watch(keys: KvKey[]) { for await (const entries of this.kv.watch(keys)) { yield entries; } } } In production, appendEvent() should also update run metadata and enqueue wakeup in same KV transaction when event scheduling event. 7. Workflow definition and DAG workflow definition immutable and versioned. interface TemporalConfig { workflowIdReuse: "reject" | "allow-after-terminal"; continueAsNewAfterEvents?: number; continueAsNewAfterBytes?: number; defaultActivityTimeoutMs: number; defaultRetry: RetryPolicy; signalRetentionMs: number; timerSweepIntervalMs: number; } interface DagNode { id: string; kind: "action" | "child" | "timer" | "signal" | "join" | "race"; dependsOn?: string[]; action?: { name: string; version: string; input: Json; compensation?: { name: string; version: string; input: Json; }; }; child?: { workflow: string; version: string; input: Json; }; timer?: { delayMs?: number; at?: number; cron?: string; }; signal?: { name: string; timeoutMs?: number; }; retry?: RetryPolicy; fallback?: string[]; raceGroup?: string; resourceCost?: { cpuMs: number; memoryMb: number; networkRequests: number; }; } interface WorkflowDefinition { name: string; version: string; temporal: TemporalConfig; nodes: DagNode[]; limits: { maxNodes: number; maxDepth: number; maxParallelism: number; maxPayloadBytes: number; maxCpuMs: number; maxMemoryMb: number; }; execute?: ( ctx: WorkflowContext, input: Json, ) => Promise; } Validation occurs before WorkflowStarted committed: function validateDag(def: WorkflowDefinition): string { if (def. nodes. length > def. limits. maxNodes) { throw new Error("MAX_NODES"); } const ids = new Set(); for (const node of def. nodes) { if (ids.has(node.id)) throw new Error(`DUPLICATE_NODE:${node.id}`); ids.add(node.id); } const indegree = new Map(); const outgoing = new Map(); for (const node of def. nodes) { indegree.set(node.id, node.dependsOn?.length ?? 0); for (const parent of node.dependsOn ?? []) { if (!ids.has(parent)) throw new Error(`UNKNOWN_DEPENDENCY:${parent}`); outgoing.set(parent, [...(outgoing.get(parent) ?? []), node.id]); } } const queue = [... def. nodes] .filter((node) => indegree.get(node.id) === 0) .map((node) => node.id); let visited = 0; while (queue. length > 0) { const id = queue.shift()!; visited++; for (const child of outgoing.get(id) ?? []) { const next = indegree.get(child)! - 1; indegree.set(child, next); if (next === 0) queue.push(child); } } if (visited!== def. nodes. length) { throw new Error("DAG_CONTAINS_CYCLE"); } if (calculateDepth(def) > def. limits. maxDepth) { throw new Error("MAX_DEPTH"); } validateResourceBounds(def); return canonicalHash(def); } graph hash stored in run. replay cannot accidentally use different graph definition. 8. Workflow context and deterministic replay workflow context exposes deterministic commands, not arbitrary effects. interface WorkflowContext { activity( nodeId: string, action: string, actionVersion: string, input: Json, options?: { retry?: RetryPolicy; compensation?: CompensationSpec; }, ): Promise; sleep(nodeId: string, delayMs: number): Promise; waitForSignal(name: string): Promise; child( nodeId: string, workflow: string, version: string, input: Json, ): Promise; continueAsNew(summary: Json): never; version(changeId: string, min: number, max: number): number; } Workflow code must use stable IDs: const orderWorkflow: WorkflowDefinition = { name: "order", version: "3.2.0", temporal: { workflowIdReuse: "reject", continueAsNewAfterEvents: 10_000, defaultActivityTimeoutMs: 30_000, defaultRetry: { maxAttempts: 5, backoffMs: 1_000, maxBackoffMs: 60_000, retryableErrors: ["TIMEOUT", "HTTP_503"], }, signalRetentionMs: 86_400_000, timerSweepIntervalMs: 60_000, }, limits: { maxNodes: 100, maxDepth: 20, maxParallelism: 16, maxPayloadBytes: 1_000_000, maxCpuMs: 300_000, maxMemoryMb: 2048, }, nodes: [ { id: "reserve-inventory", kind: "action", action: { name: "inventory.reserve", version: "2.0.0", input: { sku: "from-input", quantity: "from-input" }, compensation: { name: "inventory.release", version: "2.0.0", input: { reservationId: "from-result:reserve-inventory" }, }, }, retry: { maxAttempts: 3, backoffMs: 500, maxBackoffMs: 10_000, retryableErrors: ["TIMEOUT", "CONFLICT"], }, fallback: ["inventory-fallback"], }, { id: "charge", kind: "action", dependsOn: ["reserve-inventory"], action: { name: "payments.authorize", version: "1.4.0", input: { orderId: "from-input" }, }, }, { id: "notify", kind: "action", dependsOn: ["charge"], action: { name: "email.send", version: "1.1.0", input: { template: "order-confirmed" }, }, }, ], async execute(ctx, input) { const version = ctx.version("payment-provider-v2", 1, 2); const reservation = await ctx.activity( "reserve-inventory", "inventory.reserve", "2.0.0", { sku: input.sku, quantity: input.quantity, }, ); const payment = await ctx.activity( "charge", version === 2 ? "payments.authorize.v2" : "payments.authorize", version === 2 ? "2.0.0" : "1.4.0", { orderId: input.orderId, reservationId: reservation.reservationId, }, ); await ctx.activity( "notify", "email.send", "1.1.0", { template: "order-confirmed", orderId: input.orderId, paymentId: payment.paymentId, }, ); return { reservation, payment }; }, }; 8.1 Replay algorithm class ReplayCursor { private readonly byCommand = new Map(); private readonly versionMarkers = new Map(); constructor( private readonly history: WorkflowEvent[], private readonly results: Map, ) { for (const event of history) { if ( event.type === "ActivityCompleted" || event.type === "ActivityFailed" ) { this.byCommand.set(event.commandId, event); } if (event.type === "VersionMarker") { this.versionMarkers.set(event.changeId, event.version); } } } activityResult(commandId: string, idempotencyKey: string): Json | undefined { const durableResult = this.results.get(idempotencyKey); if (durableResult !== undefined) return durableResult; const event = this.byCommand.get(commandId); if (event?.type === "ActivityCompleted") { return this.results.get(event.idempotencyKey); } return undefined; } wasScheduled(commandId: string): boolean { return this.history.some( (e) => e.type === "ActivityScheduled" && e.commandId === commandId, ); } version(changeId: string, min: number, max: number): number { const existing = this.versionMarkers.get(changeId); if (existing !== undefined) return existing; // The first execution chooses max. That choice is recorded as an event. return max; } } class YieldWorkflow extends Error { constructor(public readonly reason: string) { super(reason); } } executor loads history and results, then reruns workflow from its initial input: async function replayAndDrive( def: WorkflowDefinition, run: RunMeta, state: DurableState, registry: AtomicRegistry, ports: PrimitivePorts, ): Promise { const history = await loadHistory(state, run. run); const results = await loadResultsForHistory(state, history); const cursor = new ReplayCursor(history, results); const ctx = new ReplayWorkflowContext( run, cursor, state, registry, ports, ); try { const output = await def.execute!(ctx, run.input); await state.appendEvent(run.run, { type: "WorkflowCompleted", resultHash: await ports.crypto.sha256( ports.crypto.canonicalJson(output), ), }); } catch (error) { if (error instanceof YieldWorkflow) { // An activity, timer, signal, or child is pending. The durable task or // timer record is already responsible for waking this run. return; } if (error instanceof ContinueAsNewSignal) { await continueAsNew(run, error.summary, state, ports); return; } await state.appendEvent(run.run, { type: "WorkflowFailed", errorCode: classifyError(error), }); } } context implementation important part: class ReplayWorkflowContext implements WorkflowContext { constructor( private readonly run: RunMeta, private readonly cursor: ReplayCursor, private readonly state: DurableState, private readonly registry: AtomicRegistry, private readonly ports: PrimitivePorts, ) {} async activity( nodeId: string, action: string, actionVersion: string, input: Json, options?: { retry?: RetryPolicy; compensation?: CompensationSpec }, ): Promise { const commandId = `${this.run.run.runId}:activity:${nodeId}`; const inputHash = await this.ports.crypto.sha256( this.ports.crypto.canonicalJson(input), ); const idempotencyKey = await this.ports.crypto.sha256( [ this.run.run.tenantId, this.run.run.workflowId, this.run.run.runId, commandId, action, actionVersion, inputHash, ].join("|"), ); const result = this.cursor.activityResult(commandId, idempotencyKey); if (result !== undefined) { return result as T; } if (!this.cursor.wasScheduled(commandId)) { await scheduleActivity({ run: this.run.run, nodeId, commandId, action, actionVersion, input, inputHash, idempotencyKey, retry: options?.retry, state: this.state, ports: this.ports, }); } throw new YieldWorkflow(`activity:${commandId}`); } async sleep(nodeId: string, delayMs: number): Promise { const timerId = `${this.run.run.runId}:timer:${nodeId}`; const history = await loadHistory(this.state, this.run.run); if (history.some( (e) => e.type === "TimerFired" && e.timerId === timerId, )) { return; } if (!history.some( (e) => e.type === "TimerScheduled" && e.timerId === timerId, )) { await scheduleTimer(this.run.run, timerId, delayMs, this.state); } throw new YieldWorkflow(`timer:${timerId}`); } version(changeId: string, min: number, max: number): number { return this.cursor.version(changeId, min, max); } continueAsNew(summary: Json): never { throw new ContinueAsNewSignal(summary); } async waitForSignal(name: string): Promise { const signal = await findUnconsumedSignal( this.state, this.run.run, name, ); if (!signal) { throw new YieldWorkflow(`signal:${name}`); } return signal.payload as T; } async child( nodeId: string, workflow: string, version: string, input: Json, ): Promise { const childRun = await ensureChildStarted( this.run.run, nodeId, workflow, version, input, this.state, this.ports, ); if (childRun.result !== undefined) { return childRun.result as T; } throw new YieldWorkflow(`child:${childRun.runId}`); } } replay never calls action merely because workflow code reaches. first checks: result KV entry by stable idempotency key; completion event by command ID; pending schedule event; only then creates new durable task. 9. Durable activity scheduling activity must write schedule event, task item, and queue message in one transaction. interface TaskEnvelope { taskId: string; queue: string; kind: "activity" | "workflow-wakeup" | "timer" | "signal"; run: RunRef; commandId?: string; action?: string; actionVersion?: string; input?: Json; inputHash?: string; idempotencyKey?: string; attempt: number; availableAt: number; signedCall?: AtomicCall; } async function scheduleActivity(args: { run: RunRef; nodeId: string; commandId: string; action: string; actionVersion: string; input: Json; inputHash: string; idempotencyKey: string; retry?: RetryPolicy; state: DurableState; ports: PrimitivePorts; }): Promise { const taskId = `${args.run.runId}:${args.commandId}`; const queue = "activities"; const event: WorkflowEvent = { type: "ActivityScheduled", commandId: args.commandId, action: args.action, actionVersion: args.actionVersion, idempotencyKey: args.idempotencyKey, inputHash: args.inputHash, }; const task: TaskEnvelope = { taskId, queue, kind: "activity", run: args.run, commandId: args.commandId, action: args.action, actionVersion: args.actionVersion, input: args.input, inputHash: args.inputHash, idempotencyKey: args.idempotencyKey, attempt: 1, availableAt: args.ports.clock.now(), }; await appendEventAndEnqueue(args. state, args. run, event, task); } queue consumer at-least-once, so claiming CAS operation: interface TaskRecord extends TaskEnvelope { status: "READY" | "RUNNING" | "COMPLETED" | "FAILED"; leaseOwner?: string; leaseUntil?: number; } async function claimTask( kv: Deno. Kv, task: TaskRecord, workerId: string, now: number, ): Promise { const key = k. task(task. queue, task. taskId); const current = await kv. get(key); if (! current. value) return false; if ( current.value.status === "COMPLETED" || ( current.value.status === "RUNNING" && (current.value.leaseUntil ?? 0) > now ) ) { return false; } const next: TaskRecord = { ...current.value, status: "RUNNING", leaseOwner: workerId, leaseUntil: now + 30_000, }; const commit = await kv. atomic() .check(current) .set(key, next) .set(k.taskLease(task.queue, task.taskId), { workerId, expiresAt: next.leaseUntil, }) .commit(); return commit. ok; } lease sweeper re-enqueues expired tasks. action result key makes duplicate task execution safe. 10. Timers timer consists of: durable timer record; TimerScheduled event; delayed KV queue message; fire CAS operation; TimerFired event; workflow wakeup. interface TimerRecord { timerId: string; run: RunRef; fireAt: number; status: "WAITING" | "FIRED" | "CANCELLED"; } async function scheduleTimer( run: RunRef, timerId: string, delayMs: number, state: DurableState, ): Promise { const fireAt = Date. now() + delayMs; const timer: TimerRecord = { timerId, run, fireAt, status: "WAITING", }; const event: WorkflowEvent = { type: "TimerScheduled", timerId, fireAt, }; const task: TaskEnvelope = { taskId: `timer:${timerId}`, queue: "timers", kind: "timer", run, attempt: 1, availableAt: fireAt, }; await appendEventAndEnqueue( state, run, event, task, { delay: delayMs }, ); } timer consumer: async function fireTimer( kv: Deno. Kv, state: DurableState, task: TaskEnvelope, ): Promise { const timerId = task. taskId. slice("timer:". length); const timerKey = k. timer( task.run.tenantId, task.run.workflowId, task.run.runId, timerId, ); const timer = await kv. get(timerKey); if (! timer. value || timer. value. status!== "WAITING") return; const next = {... timer. value, status: "FIRED" as const }; const commit = await kv. atomic() .check(timer) .set(timerKey, next) .enqueue({ kind: "workflow-wakeup", run: task.run, reason: "timer", timerId, }) .commit(); if (! commit. ok) return; await state. appendEvent(task. run, { type: "TimerFired", timerId, }); } For recurring Quartz-like schedules: store recurrence specification in workflow; materialize next occurrence as timer; on firing, compute next occurrence deterministically; enqueue another delayed timer; use top-level Deno. cron() job to scan for overdue timer records and repair missed queue deliveries. Deno. cron( "workflow-timer-reconciler", "* * * * *", async () => { await reconcileOverdueTimers(); }, ); cron job repair mechanism. timer record remains source of truth. 11. Signals external durable inputs, separate from workflow process. Signal ingestion allocates sequence using CAS head: interface SignalRecord { signalId: string; workflowId: string; name: string; payload: Json; receivedAt: number; consumedByRunId?: string; consumedAt?: number; } async function deliverSignal( kv: Deno. Kv, tenantId: string, workflowId: string, signal: SignalRecord, ): Promise { const idKey = k. signalById(tenantId, workflowId, signal. signalId); const existing = await kv. get(idKey); if (existing. value) return; // duplicate client delivery const headKey = k. signalHead(tenantId, workflowId); for (;;) { const head = await kv.get<{ nextSeq: number }>(headKey); const seq = head.value?.nextSeq ?? 0; const commit = await kv.atomic() .check(existing) .check(head) .set(idKey, signal) .set(k.signal(tenantId, workflowId, seq), signal) .set(headKey, { nextSeq: seq + 1 }) .enqueue({ kind: "signal", tenantId, workflowId, signalId: signal.signalId, signalSeq: seq, }) .commit(); if (commit.ok) return; } } signal endpoint must: authenticate caller; check capability such as signal: workflow/{workflowId}/{name}; verify signal ID for deduplication; append to signal stream; enqueue workflow wakeup. kv. watch() can watch workflow signal head, but signal handler must list signal stream from last consumed sequence. cannot assume each watch notification represents one signal. 12. Child workflows Child creation durable transaction: async function ensureChildStarted( parent: RunRef, nodeId: string, workflow: string, version: string, input: Json, state: DurableState, ports: PrimitivePorts, ): Promise<{ runId: string; result?: Json }> { const childWorkflowId = `${parent.workflowId}/${parent.runId}/child/${nodeId}`; const childRunId = await ports. crypto. sha256( `${childWorkflowId}|${version}`, ); const child: RunMeta = { run: { tenantId: parent.tenantId, workflowId: childWorkflowId, runId: childRunId, }, status: "RUNNING", definitionVersion: version, graphHash: await resolveGraphHash(workflow, version), input, parentRunId: parent.runId, createdAt: ports.clock.now(), updatedAt: ports.clock.now(), }; const existing = await state. get( k.runMeta(parent.tenantId, childWorkflowId, childRunId), ); if (! existing. value) { await atomicCreateChildAndWakeParent( parent, child, nodeId, state, ); } const childStatus = await state. get( k.runMeta(parent.tenantId, childWorkflowId, childRunId), ); if ( childStatus.value?.status === "COMPLETED" ) { return { runId: childRunId, result: await loadWorkflowResult(child.run, state), }; } return { runId: childRunId }; } child has its own event history and can be resumed or replaced independently. parent waits for durable child-completion event, not live process response. 13. Parallel DAG execution, fan-in, fallback, and races 13.1 Fan-out/fan-in For each node, maintain durable node record: interface NodeState { nodeId: string; status: "PENDING" | "READY" | "RUNNING" | "COMPLETED" | "FAILED"; dependencyCount: number; completedDependencies: number; attempts: number; resultRef?: string; errorCode?: string; } When node completes: append NodeCompleted; update dependent node counters in same CAS transaction; enqueue each newly ready node; cap number of concurrently leased tasks by durable semaphore or tenant quota. fan-in node becomes ready only when all required predecessor node states COMPLETED. 13.2 Retries retry scheduler computes: function retryDelay(policy: RetryPolicy, attempt: number): number { return Math. min( policy.maxBackoffMs, policy.backoffMs * 2 ** Math.max(0, attempt - 1), ); } Do not use unrecorded random jitter in workflow code. If jitter required, generate in runtime and record chosen delay in RetryScheduled event, or derive deterministically from command ID. retry uses: same logical commandId; same idempotency key for side effect; new attempt number in task record; new scheduled retry event. 13.3 Fallback branches fallback activated only after primary reaches terminal retry decision. fallback branch normal DAG node and persisted as such: primary failed -> RetryScheduled -> RetryExhausted -> fallback node READY -> fallback action scheduled workflow must not infer fallback activation merely from missing task. 13.4 Race conditions For multiple agents competing for one resource: interface LockRecord { owner: string; acquiredAt: number; expiresAt: number; winnerResultRef?: string; } Acquire with: async function tryAcquireRace( kv: Deno. Kv, tenantId: string, lockName: string, owner: string, now: number, ): Promise { const key = k. raceLock(tenantId, lockName); const current = await kv. get(key); if ( current.value && current.value.expiresAt > now && current.value.owner !== owner ) { return false; } const record: LockRecord = { owner, acquiredAt: now, expiresAt: now + 30_000, }; return ( await kv.atomic() .check(current) .set(key, record) .commit() ). ok; } All competitors execute same CAS. Exactly one sees success. Losers either: wait for winner’s result; execute configured fallback; retry after lock expiration. lock not workflow result. winner must still write durable result and completion event. 14. Saga and compensation Core maintains compensation stack in run state: interface CompensationSpec { name: string; version: string; input: Json; } interface SagaEntry { completedCommandId: string; compensation: CompensationSpec; } When activity with compensation completes, Core appends: { type: "CompensationScheduled", compensationId: "...", action: "inventory. release" } On failure: stop forward DAG scheduling; reverse completed compensation stack; schedule each compensation as normal idempotent task; record compensation results; mark workflow FAILED, COMPENSATED, or COMPENSATION_FAILED. Compensations must be independently retryable and must not rely on failed agent’s memory. 15. Continue-as-new Continue-as-new prevents unbounded history growth. class ContinueAsNewSignal extends Error { constructor(public readonly summary: Json) { super("CONTINUE_AS_NEW"); } } transaction: validate and serialize summary; create new run ID; write new run metadata; append ContinueAsNew to old run; mark old run CONTINUED; enqueue new run; update workflow metadata to point to new run. summary must contain only state necessary to continue. must not include entire prior history. async function continueAsNew( oldRun: RunMeta, summary: Json, state: DurableState, ports: PrimitivePorts, ): Promise { const nextRunId = await ports. crypto. sha256( `${oldRun.run.runId}|continue-as-new|${ports.clock.now()}`, ); const next: RunMeta = { ...oldRun, run: { ...oldRun.run, runId: nextRunId, }, status: "RUNNING", input: summary, parentRunId: oldRun.run.runId, continueAsNewFrom: oldRun.run.runId, createdAt: ports.clock.now(), updatedAt: ports.clock.now(), }; await atomicContinueAsNew(oldRun, next, summary, state, ports); } old run remains queryable and auditable. workflow ID remains stable while run ID changes. 16. Workflow versioning run pins: workflow name workflow definition version graph hash action versions New deployments do not change definition used by existing runs. For in-place workflow code changes: const version = ctx. version("new-payment-provider", 1, 2); first execution records VersionMarker. Every replay returns recorded value. Old runs therefore continue down old branch while new runs use new branch. definition may be retired only after: no active run references; all continue-as-new transitions have migrated; migration handler exists for any persisted summary format. 17. Agent processes, Workers, Sandbox, and WebTransport 17.1 Agent spawning Gateway owns agent lifecycle. Agents replaceable executors, not workflow owners. async function spawnAgent( spec: AgentSpec, ): Promise { const command = new Deno. Command(Deno. execPath(), { args: [ "run", "--allow-net=127.0.0.1", "--deny-read", "--deny-write", "agent.ts", ], env: { AGENT_ID: spec.agentId, AGENT_PORT: String(spec.port), ACTION_SET: spec.actionSet.join(","), }, stdout: "piped", stderr: "piped", }); const child = command. spawn(); return { agentId: spec.agentId, pid: child.pid, async close() { child.kill("SIGTERM"); }, }; } exact Deno permissions generated from action sandbox profile, not supplied by agent. 17.2 CPU isolation For CPU-bound but non-hostile code: const worker = new Worker( new URL("./cpu-worker. ts", import. meta. url). href, { type: "module", name: `activity-${task.taskId}`, }, ); worker. postMessage({ taskId: task. taskId, input: task. input, }); Worker provides event-loop and memory isolation but should not be treated as security boundary equivalent to process or microVM. 17.3 MicroVM execution Use current Sandbox package: import { Sandbox } from "@deno/sandbox"; async function executeInSandbox( code: string, profile: SandboxProfile, ): Promise { await using sandbox = await Sandbox. create({ allowNet: profile.allowNet ?? [], memoryMb: profile.memoryMb ?? 768, timeout: `${Math.ceil(profile.timeoutMs / 1000)}s`, env: profile.env, }); await sandbox. writeTextFile("/tmp/action. ts", code); const result = await sandbox. sh`deno run --no-prompt /tmp/action.ts`; return JSON. parse(result. stdout); } @deno/sandbox creates isolated Linux microVM with configurable network allowlists, memory, timeout, environment, and labels. appropriate for untrusted custom actions. 17.4 WebTransport and mTLS Deno WebTransport server has following shape: const endpoint = new Deno. QuicEndpoint({ hostname: "0.0.0.0", port: 4433, }); const listener = endpoint. listen({ cert: await Deno. readTextFile("./gateway. crt"), key: await Deno. readTextFile("./gateway. key"), alpnProtocols: ["h3"], }); for await (const incoming of listener) { const conn = await incoming. accept(); const transport = await Deno. upgradeWebTransport(conn); await handleAgentTransport(transport); } For production mTLS: terminate QUIC/TLS at certificate-validating Gateway or QUIC proxy; validate client certificate chain, SAN, tenant, agent ID, expiration, and revocation; map certificate identity to agent capability policy; forward signed call envelope to agent; require agent to return signed result envelope. Gateway should not rely solely on transport certificate. Every atomic call also carries its own signed authorization context. For transports support HTTP headers, propagate: Authorization: Bearer X-Session-Id: X-Workflow-Id: X-Run-Id: Traceparent: If particular WebTransport client API does not expose arbitrary request headers, send same signed envelope as first message on bidirectional stream and reject stream until validates. 18. Resumable sessions Session state not workflow state. /workflows/... workflow execution truth /sessions/{tenant}/{sid} agent/user session metadata /sessions/.../events conversation/tool/session events /refreshTokens/... hashed refresh-token records 18.1 Session metadata interface SessionMeta { sessionId: string; tenantId: string; subject: string; status: "ACTIVE" | "REVOKED" | "EXPIRED"; currentAgentId?: string; lastEventSeq: number; createdAt: number; expiresAt: number; } interface SessionEvent { seq: number; type: "USER_MESSAGE" | "AGENT_MESSAGE" | "TOOL_CALL" | "TOOL_RESULT"; payload: Json; at: number; } 18.2 Refresh flow client presents refresh token. Gateway hashes with injected crypto provider. looks up hash in KV. checks session, tenant, expiry, rotation state, and revocation. atomically revokes old refresh token and writes new hash. issues short-lived signed access token. access token sent with every call. Only token hashes persisted. Refresh tokens never stored in plaintext. 18.3 Agent replacement When agent dies: Gateway marks its lease expired; harness finds session’s currentAgentId; starts new agent process or Sandbox; replays session events from lastAcknowledgedSeq; sends agent all currently pending workflow signals; resumes task execution using same workflow/run IDs; any in-flight atomic task retried using its original idempotency key. new agent does not receive mutable in-memory workflow object. receives reconstructed session projection and signed, capability-scoped task envelopes. 19. Gateway and audit security flow For every action: 1. mTLS authenticates process or agent. 2. Gateway authenticates access token. 3. Gateway verifies workflow/run ownership. 4. Harness creates stable command and idempotency key. 5. Registry resolves action and policy. 6. Capability token checked against action/resource/time/tenant. 7. Canonical call envelope signed. 8. Runtime executes in selected Worker/process/microVM. 9. Result stored by idempotency key. 10. Workflow completion event appended. 11. Audit record written to separate audit storage. 12. OpenTelemetry span ended. Audit records should contain: tenant; subject and certificate identity; workflow/run/command; action and action version; capability ID or token hash; input hash, not secret input; result hash; attempt; sandbox profile; start/end time; outcome; trace ID. workflow context must have no audit() method and no read access to audit database. Audit data out-of-context and cannot affect deterministic replay. For high-assurance deployments, audit writes should be fail-closed for privileged actions: action result -> audit append -> publish workflow completion For ordinary actions, audit can be written asynchronously, but audit_pending metric and durable retry record required. 20. Harness entry point export interface HarnessOptions { state: DurableState; registry: AtomicRegistry; definitions: WorkflowDefinitionStore; ports: PrimitivePorts; tenantPolicy: TenantPolicy; } export class DurableWorkflowHarness { constructor(private readonly options: HarnessOptions) {} async start( tenantId: string, workflowId: string, definitionName: string, definitionVersion: string, input: Json, ): Promise { const def = await this.options.definitions.get( definitionName, definitionVersion, ); const graphHash = validateDag(def); validateInputSize(input, def.limits.maxPayloadBytes); await this.options.tenantPolicy.authorizeStart(tenantId, def); const runId = await stableRunId( tenantId, workflowId, definitionVersion, this.options.ports, ); const run: RunRef = { tenantId, workflowId, runId }; await createWorkflowAndInitialRun({ run, definition: def, graphHash, input, state: this.options.state, ports: this.options.ports, }); return run; } async signal( tenantId: string, workflowId: string, signalId: string, name: string, payload: Json, capabilityToken: string, ): Promise { await this.options.ports.permissions.verify( capabilityToken, { resource: `signal:${workflowId}:${name}`, operations: ["send"], }, { tenantId, workflowId, runId: "", action: "workflow.signal", actionVersion: "1", commandId: signalId, idempotencyKey: signalId, input: payload, inputHash: "", capabilityToken, issuedAt: Date.now(), expiresAt: Date.now() + 30_000, nonce: signalId, signature: "", }, ); await deliverSignal( (this.options.state as DenoDurableState)["kv"], tenantId, workflowId, { signalId, workflowId, name, payload, receivedAt: this.options.ports.clock.now(), }, ); } async runWorker(workerId: string): Promise { await listenForTasks( this.options.state, async (task) => { await executeTask( workerId, task, this.options.state, this.options.registry, this.options.ports, ); }, ); } } production implementation should avoid private KV access shown in signal() by adding first-class DurableState. appendSignal() method. important point harness entry point depends on interfaces, while Deno adapter contains all concrete KV operations. 21. Testing strategy Each primitive and layer independently testable. Atomic action tests Use fake ports: const fakeNetwork = new FakeNetwork(); const fakeClock = new FakeClock(1_700_000_000_000); const fakeSigner = new DeterministicEd25519Signer(); const fakePermissions = new AllowListPermissions(); const fakeExecution = new InlineExecution(); Test: capability denial; timeout; input schema rejection; duplicate idempotency key; provider retry; action result replay; secret redaction. Core replay tests Persist fixture history, then run workflow twice: const first = await driveWorkflow(definition, fakeState, input); const second = await replayWorkflow(definition, fakeState, input); assertEquals(first. decisions, second. decisions); assertEquals(fakeNetwork. calls, 1); same action must not be called during replay after its result exists. Crash tests Inject failures at every boundary: before task claim after task claim after external action before result write after result write before event append after event append before queue enqueue after queue enqueue Expected behavior: expired leases reclaimed; duplicate queue messages harmless; duplicate external calls use same idempotency key; completed results reused; workflow events remain append-only; new agent resumes from KV. Determinism tests Reject or lint workflow code uses: Date; Math. random; direct fetch; direct crypto. randomUUID; process environment; uncontrolled iteration over object keys; non-versioned action resolution. workflow DSL should expose deterministic now, random, activity, sleep, signal, and child APIs backed by recorded events. architecture gives harness Temporal-like durable execution while keeping every side-effect boundary replaceable: Deno KV can be replaced with another DurableState, WebCrypto with HSM signer, Workers with process runner, Sandbox with another microVM backend, and WebTransport with another authenticated transport without changing workflow semantics. Harness Core // harness/types. ts type WorkflowId = string; type RunId = string; type ActivityId = string; type SignalName = string; type TimerId = string; interface WorkflowState { id: WorkflowId; runId: RunId; status: "pending" | "running" | "completed" | "failed" | "terminated"; startedAt: number; completedAt?: number; parentExecution?: { workflowId: WorkflowId; runId: RunId }; continueAsNew?: { nextRunId: RunId; reason: string }; dag: DagDefinition; version: number; retryState: RetryState; } interface DagDefinition { nodes: Map; edges: DagEdge[]; timeout?: number; } interface DagNode { id: string; activity: string; // references AtomicRegistry input: Record; timeout?: number; retryPolicy?: RetryPolicy; compensation?: string; // activity for rollback isFanOut?: boolean; isFanIn?: boolean; } interface DagEdge { from: string; to: string; condition?: string; // expression for conditional branching } interface RetryPolicy { maxAttempts: number; backoffCoefficient: number; initialInterval: number; maxInterval: number; } interface RetryState { attempt: number; lastFailure?: string; nextRetryAt?: number; } // Core Harness Interface WorkflowHarness { start(workflowId: WorkflowId, dag: DagDefinition, input: unknown): Promise; signal(workflowId: WorkflowId, signal: SignalName, payload: unknown): Promise; query(workflowId: WorkflowId, query: string): Promise; terminate(workflowId: WorkflowId, reason: string): Promise; continueAsNew(workflowId: WorkflowId, input: unknown, version?: number): Promise; } 2. Atomic Registry // registry/types. ts interface AtomicPrimitive { name: string; version: number; execute: (ctx: AtomicContext, input: unknown) => Promise; sandbox: SandboxProfile; timeout: number; retryPolicy: RetryPolicy; idempotencyKey?: (input: unknown) => string; requiredCapabilities: Capability[]; } interface SandboxProfile { type: "worker" | "deno-sandbox" | "isolated-process"; permissions: { net?: string[]; // allowed hosts read?: string[]; // allowed paths write?: string[]; // allowed paths env?: string[]; // allowed env vars run?: boolean; // subprocess execution }; memoryMb?: number; } interface Capability { type: "crypto" | "network" | "schedule" | "execution" | "memory" | "storage"; action: string; constraints?: Record; } interface AtomicContext { workflowId: WorkflowId; runId: RunId; activityId: ActivityId; idempotencyKey: string; capabilityToken: CapabilityToken; traceId: string; spanId: string; signal: AbortSignal; } interface AtomicResult { idempotencyKey: string; result: unknown; completedAt: number; duration: number; status: "success" | "failure" | "timeout" | "cancelled"; auditHash: string; } interface AtomicRegistry { register(primitive: AtomicPrimitive): void; get(name: string, version?: number): AtomicPrimitive | undefined; list(): AtomicPrimitive[]; unregister(name: string, version?: number): void; } 3. Durable State Layer // state/types. ts interface DurableStateStore { // Event operations appendEvent(workflowId: WorkflowId, runId: RunId, event: WorkflowEvent): Promise; getEvents(workflowId: WorkflowId, runId: RunId, fromSequence?: number): AsyncIterable; getEventsSync(workflowId: WorkflowId, runId: RunId): Promise; // State operations getWorkflowState(workflowId: WorkflowId): Promise; setWorkflowState(workflowId: WorkflowId, state: WorkflowState): Promise; // Signal operations enqueueSignal(workflowId: WorkflowId, signal: SignalName, payload: unknown): Promise; dequeueSignals(workflowId: WorkflowId): Promise; // Timer operations scheduleTimer(workflowId: WorkflowId, timerId: TimerId, delayMs: number, payload?: unknown): Promise; cancelTimer(workflowId: WorkflowId, timerId: TimerId): Promise; // Idempotency getResultForKey(workflowId: WorkflowId, idempotencyKey: string): Promise; setResultForKey(workflowId: WorkflowId, idempotencyKey: string, result: AtomicResult): Promise; // Child workflow spawnChild(parentId: WorkflowId, childId: WorkflowId, dag: DagDefinition, input: unknown): Promise; // Session management getSession(sessionId: string): Promise; updateSession(sessionId: string, state: SessionState): Promise; // Watch watchWorkflow(workflowId: WorkflowId): ReadableStream; watchSignals(workflowId: WorkflowId): ReadableStream; } interface WorkflowEvent { sequence: number; type: WorkflowEventType; timestamp: number; payload: unknown; idempotencyKey?: string; activityId?: ActivityId; childWorkflowId?: WorkflowId; } type WorkflowEventType = | "workflow_started" | "activity_scheduled" | "activity_completed" | "activity_failed" | "timer_started" | "timer_fired" | "signal_received" | "signal_sent" | "child_workflow_spawned" | "child_workflow_completed" | "continue_as_new" | "workflow_completed" | "workflow_failed" | "compensation_started" | "compensation_completed"; interface SignalEvent { signal: SignalName; payload: unknown; timestamp: number; sequence: number; } interface SessionState { sessionId: string; workflowId: WorkflowId; agentId: string; status: "active" | "suspended" | "terminated"; lastHeartbeat: number; refreshToken: string; capabilities: Capability[]; } 4. Execution Runtime // runtime/types. ts interface ExecutionRuntime { // Activity execution executeActivity(primitive: AtomicPrimitive, ctx: AtomicContext, input: unknown): Promise; // Worker management spawnWorker(spec: WorkerSpec): Promise; spawnSandbox(spec: SandboxSpec): Promise; // Parallel execution fanOut(tasks: (() => Promise)[]): Promise; race(tasks: (() => Promise)[]): Promise; // Circuit breaking circuitBreaker(name: string, options: CircuitBreakerOptions): CircuitBreaker; } interface WorkerSpec { moduleUrl: string; permissions: SandboxProfile["permissions"]; memoryMb?: number; timeout?: number; } interface WorkerHandle { id: string; postMessage(msg: unknown): void; onMessage(): AsyncIterable; terminate(): Promise; } interface SandboxSpec { allowNet?: string[]; memoryMb?: number; timeout?: string; region?: string; env?: Record; } interface SandboxHandle { id: string; run(command: string): Promise<{ stdout: string; stderr: string; exitCode: number }>; upload(path: string, content: Uint8Array): Promise; close(): Promise; } interface CircuitBreakerOptions { failureThreshold: number; resetTimeout: number; monitorInterval: number; } interface CircuitBreaker { execute(fn: () => Promise): Promise; getState(): "closed" | "open" | "half-open"; } Deno KV Key Schema /workflows/{id}/state → WorkflowState /workflows/{id}/run/{run_id}/events/{seq} → WorkflowEvent /workflows/{id}/run/{run_id}/event_cursor → number (next sequence) /workflows/{id}/signals/{signal_name}/{ts} → SignalEvent /workflows/{id}/signal_cursor → number (next signal sequence) /workflows/{id}/timers/{timer_id} → TimerState /workflows/{id}/results/{idempotency_key} → AtomicResult /workflows/{id}/children/{child_id} → ChildWorkflowRef /sessions/{session_id} → SessionState /sessions/{session_id}/events/{seq} → SessionEvent /agents/{agent_id}/state → AgentState /agents/{agent_id}/certs → mTLS certificate /tasks/{queue}/items/{item_id} → TaskItem /audit/{workflow_id}/{ts}/{seq} → AuditEntry /capabilities/{token_id} → CapabilityToken /locks/{resource}/{workflow_id} → LockState /registry/{name}/v{version} → AtomicPrimitive metadata Atomic Primitive Registry Design // registry/atomic_registry. ts import { Sandbox } from "@deno/sandbox"; interface RegisteredPrimitive extends AtomicPrimitive { registeredAt: number; moduleHash: string; signature: Uint8Array; } class AtomicPrimitiveRegistryImpl implements AtomicRegistry { private primitives = new Map>(); private kv: Deno. Kv; constructor(kv: Deno. Kv) { this.kv = kv; } register(primitive: AtomicPrimitive): void { const versions = this.primitives.get(primitive.name) ?? new Map(); versions.set(primitive.version, { ...primitive, registeredAt: Date.now(), moduleHash: "", // computed from module source signature: new Uint8Array(), // Ed25519 signature }); this.primitives.set(primitive.name, versions); } get(name: string, version?: number): AtomicPrimitive | undefined { const versions = this.primitives.get(name); if (!versions) return undefined; if (version !== undefined) return versions.get(version); // Return latest version const maxVersion = Math.max(...versions.keys()); return versions.get(maxVersion); } list(): AtomicPrimitive[] { const result: AtomicPrimitive[] = []; for (const versions of this.primitives.values()) { const maxVersion = Math.max(...versions.keys()); const latest = versions.get(maxVersion); if (latest) result.push(latest); } return result; } unregister(name: string, version?: number): void { if (version !== undefined) { this.primitives.get(name)?.delete(version); } else { this.primitives.delete(name); } } } // Example: Registering custom atomic action const httpPrimitive: AtomicPrimitive = { name: "http_request", version: 1, execute: async (ctx: AtomicContext, input: unknown) => { const { url, method, headers, body } = input as { url: string; method: string; headers?: Record; body?: string; }; const response = await fetch(url, { method, headers: { ...headers, "X-Idempotency-Key": ctx.idempotencyKey }, body, signal: ctx.signal, }); return { status: response.status, body: await response.text() }; }, sandbox: { type: "deno-sandbox", permissions: { net: ["*"], }, memoryMb: 256, }, timeout: 30000, retryPolicy: { maxAttempts: 3, backoffCoefficient: 2, initialInterval: 1000, maxInterval: 10000, }, idempotencyKey: (input: unknown) => { const { url, method, body } = input as { url: string; method: string; body?: string }; // Deterministic key from input const hash = crypto.subtle.digest( "SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(`${method}:${url}:${body ?? ""}`) ); return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(hash))); }, requiredCapabilities: [ { type: "network", action: "fetch", constraints: { hosts: ["*"] } }, ], }; registry. register(httpPrimitive); Durable Replay Mechanism // replay/replay_engine. ts interface ReplayState { currentSequence: number; activityResults: Map; timerStates: Map; signalBuffer: SignalEvent[]; } class ReplayEngine { private kv: Deno. Kv; private registry: AtomicRegistry; private runtime: ExecutionRuntime; private tracer: Tracer; constructor(kv: Deno. Kv, registry: AtomicRegistry, runtime: ExecutionRuntime) { this.kv = kv; this.registry = registry; this.runtime = runtime; } async replay(workflowId: WorkflowId, runId: RunId): Promise { const state: ReplayState = { currentSequence: 0, activityResults: new Map(), timerStates: new Map(), signalBuffer: [], }; // Load all events for this run const events: WorkflowEvent[] = []; for await (const event of this.kv.list({ prefix: ["workflows", workflowId, "run", runId, "events"], })) { events.push(event.value); } // Rebuild state from events for (const event of events) { this.applyEvent(state, event); } return state; } private applyEvent(state: ReplayState, event: WorkflowEvent): void { state.currentSequence = event.sequence; switch (event.type) { case "activity_completed": { const { idempotencyKey, result } = event.payload as { idempotencyKey: string; result: unknown; }; // Cache result so replay doesn't re-execute state.activityResults.set(idempotencyKey, { idempotencyKey, result, completedAt: event.timestamp, duration: 0, status: "success", auditHash: "", }); break; } case "activity_failed": { const { idempotencyKey, error } = event.payload as { idempotencyKey: string; error: string; }; state.activityResults.set(idempotencyKey, { idempotencyKey, result: null, completedAt: event.timestamp, duration: 0, status: "failure", auditHash: "", }); break; } case "timer_fired": { const { timerId } = event.payload as { timerId: string }; state.timerStates.set(timerId, { status: "fired", firedAt: event.timestamp }); break; } case "signal_received": { state.signalBuffer.push(event.payload as SignalEvent); break; } } } // Execute activity with replay check async executeActivityWithReplay( workflowId: WorkflowId, runId: RunId, replayState: ReplayState, primitiveName: string, input: unknown, ctx: Omit ): Promise { const primitive = this.registry.get(primitiveName); if (!primitive) throw new Error(`Primitive ${primitiveName} not registered`); // Generate idempotency key const idempotencyKey = primitive.idempotencyKey ? primitive.idempotencyKey(input) : await this.defaultIdempotencyKey(primitiveName, input); // Check if already executed (replay) const cached = replayState.activityResults.get(idempotencyKey); if (cached) { // Return cached result without re-executing await this.tracer.span("replay.skip", { workflowId, runId, activityId: primitiveName, idempotencyKey, }); return cached.result; } // Check KV for result (might be from a previous partial execution) const kvResult = await this.kv.get([ "workflows", workflowId, "results", idempotencyKey, ]); if (kvResult.value) { replayState.activityResults.set(idempotencyKey, kvResult.value); return kvResult.value.result; } // Execute the activity const fullCtx: AtomicContext = { ...ctx, idempotencyKey, }; const result = await this.runtime.executeActivity(primitive, fullCtx, input); // Persist result atomically await this.kv.atomic() .set(["workflows", workflowId, "results", idempotencyKey], result) .commit(); // Append event await this.appendEvent(workflowId, runId, { sequence: replayState.currentSequence + 1, type: "activity_completed", timestamp: Date.now(), payload: { idempotencyKey, result: result.result }, idempotencyKey, activityId: primitiveName, }); replayState.currentSequence++; replayState.activityResults.set(idempotencyKey, result); return result.result; } private async defaultIdempotencyKey(primitiveName: string, input: unknown): Promise { const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest( "SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(`${primitiveName}:${JSON.stringify(input)}`) ); return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(hash))); } private async appendEvent( workflowId: WorkflowId, runId: RunId, event: WorkflowEvent ): Promise { await this.kv.set( ["workflows", workflowId, "run", runId, "events", event.sequence], event ); } } Signals, Timers, and Child Workflows // primitives/signals_timers. ts class SignalHandler { private kv: Deno. Kv; constructor(kv: Deno. Kv) { this.kv = kv; } // Send signal to workflow async sendSignal( workflowId: WorkflowId, signal: SignalName, payload: unknown ): Promise { const sequence = await this.getNextSignalSequence(workflowId); const signalEvent: SignalEvent = { signal, payload, timestamp: Date.now(), sequence, }; await this.kv.atomic() .set(["workflows", workflowId, "signals", signal, Date.now()], signalEvent) .commit(); // Watch mechanism will pick this up } // Watch for signals using KV watch async *watchSignals(workflowId: WorkflowId): AsyncGenerator { const stream = this.kv.watch([["workflows", workflowId, "signals"]]); for await (const entries of stream) { for (const entry of entries) { if (entry.value) { yield entry.value as SignalEvent; } } } } private async getNextSignalSequence(workflowId: WorkflowId): Promise { const cursorKey = ["workflows", workflowId, "signal_cursor"]; const result = await this.kv.get(cursorKey); const next = (result.value ?? 0) + 1; await this.kv.set(cursorKey, next); return next; } } class TimerManager { private kv: Deno. Kv; constructor(kv: Deno. Kv) { this.kv = kv; } // Schedule timer using KV queue with delay async scheduleTimer( workflowId: WorkflowId, timerId: TimerId, delayMs: number, payload?: unknown ): Promise { const timerState: TimerState = { timerId, workflowId, scheduledAt: Date.now(), fireAt: Date.now() + delayMs, status: "scheduled", payload, }; // Persist timer state await this.kv.set(["workflows", workflowId, "timers", timerId], timerState); // Enqueue delayed message await this.kv.enqueue( { type: "timer_fired", workflowId, timerId, payload, }, { delay: delayMs, keysIfUndelivered: [ ["workflows", workflowId, "timers", timerId, "undelivered"], ], } ); } async cancelTimer(workflowId: WorkflowId, timerId: TimerId): Promise { const timerKey = ["workflows", workflowId, "timers", timerId]; const timer = await this.kv.get(timerKey); if (timer.value && timer.value.status === "scheduled") { await this.kv.set(timerKey, { ...timer.value, status: "cancelled" }); } } // Listen for timer events async listenForTimers(handler: (msg: TimerMessage) => Promise): Promise { await this.kv.listenQueue(async (msg: unknown) => { if (isTimerMessage(msg)) { // Verify timer not cancelled const timerKey = ["workflows", msg.workflowId, "timers", msg.timerId]; const timer = await this.kv.get(timerKey); if (timer.value?.status === "scheduled") { await this.kv.set(timerKey, { ...timer.value, status: "fired" }); await handler(msg); } } }); } } interface TimerState { timerId: TimerId; workflowId: WorkflowId; scheduledAt: number; fireAt: number; status: "scheduled" | "fired" | "cancelled"; payload?: unknown; } interface TimerMessage { type: "timer_fired"; workflowId: WorkflowId; timerId: TimerId; payload?: unknown; } function isTimerMessage(msg: unknown): msg TimerMessage { return (msg as TimerMessage). type === "timer_fired"; } // Child Workflow Spawning class ChildWorkflowManager { private kv: Deno. Kv; private harness: WorkflowHarness; constructor(kv: Deno. Kv, harness: WorkflowHarness) { this.kv = kv; this.harness = harness; } async spawnChild( parentId: WorkflowId, parentRunId: RunId, dag: DagDefinition, input: unknown ): Promise<{ childId: WorkflowId; runId: RunId }> { const childId = `${parentId}:child:${crypto.randomUUID()}`; const runId = await this.harness.start(childId, dag, input); // Link child to parent await this.kv.set( ["workflows", parentId, "children", childId], { childId, runId, status: "running", spawnedAt: Date.now() } ); // Watch child completion this.watchChildCompletion(parentId, parentRunId, childId, runId); return { childId, runId }; } private async watchChildCompletion( parentId: WorkflowId, parentRunId: RunId, childId: WorkflowId, childRunId: RunId ): Promise { const stream = this.kv.watch([ ["workflows", childId, "state"], ]); for await (const entries of stream) { const state = entries[0]?.value as WorkflowState | null; if (state?.status === "completed" || state?.status === "failed") { // Append event to parent const events = this.kv.list({ prefix: ["workflows", parentId, "run", parentRunId, "events"], }); let maxSeq = 0; for await (const e of events) { maxSeq = Math.max(maxSeq, e.value.sequence); } await this.kv.set( ["workflows", parentId, "run", parentRunId, "events", maxSeq + 1], { sequence: maxSeq + 1, type: "child_workflow_completed", timestamp: Date.now(), payload: { childId, childRunId, status: state.status, result: state.status === "completed" ? state : undefined, }, childWorkflowId: childId, } ); break; } } } } Concrete Code Skeletons Harness Entry Point // harness/entry. ts import { Sandbox } from "@deno/sandbox"; const kv = await Deno. openKv(); class WorkflowHarnessImpl implements WorkflowHarness { private registry: AtomicPrimitiveRegistryImpl; private replayEngine: ReplayEngine; private signalHandler: SignalHandler; private timerManager: TimerManager; private childManager: ChildWorkflowManager; private runtime: ExecutionRuntime; private security: ZeroTrustSecurity; private tracer: Tracer; constructor() { this.registry = new AtomicPrimitiveRegistryImpl(kv); this.runtime = new DenoExecutionRuntime(); this.replayEngine = new ReplayEngine(kv, this.registry, this.runtime); this.signalHandler = new SignalHandler(kv); this.timerManager = new TimerManager(kv); this.childManager = new ChildWorkflowManager(kv, this); this.security = new ZeroTrustSecurity(kv); this.tracer = new OpenTelemetryTracer(); } async start(workflowId: WorkflowId, dag: DagDefinition, input: unknown): Promise { // Validate DAG this.validateDag(dag); const runId = crypto.randomUUID(); const state: WorkflowState = { id: workflowId, runId, status: "pending", startedAt: Date.now(), dag, version: 1, retryState: { attempt: 0 }, }; await kv.atomic() .set(["workflows", workflowId, "state"], state) .set(["workflows", workflowId, "run", runId, "event_cursor"], 0) .set(["workflows", workflowId, "run", runId, "events", 0], { sequence: 0, type: "workflow_started", timestamp: Date.now(), payload: { input }, }) .commit(); // Start execution this.executeWorkflow(workflowId, runId, dag, input); return runId; } async signal(workflowId: WorkflowId, signal: SignalName, payload: unknown): Promise { await this.signalHandler.sendSignal(workflowId, signal, payload); } async query(workflowId: WorkflowId, query: string): Promise { const state = await kv.get(["workflows", workflowId, "state"]); // Implement query handlers based on workflow type throw new Error("Not implemented"); } async terminate(workflowId: WorkflowId, reason: string): Promise { const state = await kv.get(["workflows", workflowId, "state"]); if (state.value) { await kv.set(["workflows", workflowId, "state"], { ...state.value, status: "terminated", completedAt: Date.now(), }); } } async continueAsNew(workflowId: WorkflowId, input: unknown, version?: number): Promise { const currentState = await kv.get(["workflows", workflowId, "state"]); if (!currentState.value) throw new Error("Workflow not found"); // Summarize context from current run const events: WorkflowEvent[] = []; for await (const event of kv.list({ prefix: ["workflows", workflowId, "run", currentState.value.runId, "events"], })) { events.push(event.value); } const summarizedContext = this.summarizeContext(events); // Create new run const newRunId = crypto.randomUUID(); const newState: WorkflowState = { ...currentState.value, runId: newRunId, status: "pending", startedAt: Date.now(), completedAt: undefined, version: version ?? currentState.value.version + 1, continueAsNew: { nextRunId: newRunId, reason: "continue-as-new" }, retryState: { attempt: 0 }, }; await kv.atomic() .set(["workflows", workflowId, "state"], newState) .set(["workflows", workflowId, "run", newRunId, "event_cursor"], 0) .set(["workflows", workflowId, "run", newRunId, "events", 0], { sequence: 0, type: "workflow_started", timestamp: Date.now(), payload: { input, previousRunId: currentState.value.runId, summarizedContext }, }) .commit(); this.executeWorkflow(workflowId, newRunId, currentState.value.dag, { ...input, _continueAsNew: summarizedContext, }); return newRunId; } private async executeWorkflow( workflowId: WorkflowId, runId: RunId, dag: DagDefinition, input: unknown ): Promise { try { await kv.set(["workflows", workflowId, "state"], { ...(await kv.get(["workflows", workflowId, "state"])).value!, status: "running", }); // Topological sort for execution order const executionOrder = this.topologicalSort(dag); const completedNodes = new Set(); const nodeResults = new Map(); for (const batch of executionOrder) { // Execute batch in parallel const promises = batch.map(async (nodeId) => { const node = dag.nodes.get(nodeId)!; // Check dependencies for (const edge of dag.edges) { if (edge.to === nodeId && !completedNodes.has(edge.from)) { throw new Error(`Dependency ${edge.from} not completed for ${nodeId}`); } } // Resolve input from dependencies const resolvedInput = this.resolveInput(node.input, nodeResults); // Execute with security check const result = await this.executeNode( workflowId, runId, node, resolvedInput ); completedNodes.add(nodeId); nodeResults.set(nodeId, result); }); await Promise.all(promises); } // Workflow completed await kv.set(["workflows", workflowId, "state"], { ...(await kv.get(["workflows", workflowId, "state"])).value!, status: "completed", completedAt: Date.now(), }); } catch (error) { // Handle failure with compensation await this.handleFailure(workflowId, runId, dag, error); } } private async executeNode( workflowId: WorkflowId, runId: RunId, node: DagNode, input: unknown ): Promise { const primitive = this.registry.get(node.activity); if (!primitive) throw new Error(`Activity ${node.activity} not registered`); // Create context with security const ctx = await this.security.createContext(workflowId, runId, primitive); // Execute with replay check const replayState = await this.replayEngine.replay(workflowId, runId); return this.replayEngine.executeActivityWithReplay( workflowId, runId, replayState, node.activity, input, ctx ); } private validateDag(dag: DagDefinition): void { // Cycle detection using DFS const visited = new Set(); const recursionStack = new Set(); const hasCycle = (nodeId: string): boolean => { visited.add(nodeId); recursionStack.add(nodeId); for (const edge of dag.edges) { if (edge.from === nodeId) { if (!visited.has(edge.to)) { if (hasCycle(edge.to)) return true; } else if (recursionStack.has(edge.to)) { return true; } } } recursionStack.delete(nodeId); return false; }; for (const nodeId of dag.nodes.keys()) { if (!visited.has(nodeId)) { if (hasCycle(nodeId)) { throw new Error("DAG contains cycle"); } } } } private topologicalSort(dag: DagDefinition): string[][] { const inDegree = new Map(); const adjacency = new Map(); for (const nodeId of dag.nodes.keys()) { inDegree.set(nodeId, 0); adjacency.set(nodeId, []); } for (const edge of dag.edges) { adjacency.get(edge.from)?.push(edge.to); inDegree.set(edge.to, (inDegree.get(edge.to) ?? 0) + 1); } const batches: string[][] = []; let currentBatch = [...dag.nodes.keys()].filter(n => inDegree.get(n) === 0); while (currentBatch.length > 0) { batches.push(currentBatch); const nextBatch: string[] = []; for (const nodeId of currentBatch) { for (const neighbor of adjacency.get(nodeId) ?? []) { const newDegree = (inDegree.get(neighbor) ?? 1) - 1; inDegree.set(neighbor, newDegree); if (newDegree === 0) nextBatch.push(neighbor); } } currentBatch = nextBatch; } return batches; } private resolveInput(template: Record, results: Map): unknown { // Resolve $ref references to previous node results const resolved: Record = {}; for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(template)) { if (typeof value === "string" && value.startsWith("$ref:")) { const refNode = value.slice(5); resolved[key] = results.get(refNode); } else { resolved[key] = value; } } return resolved; } private summarizeContext(events: WorkflowEvent[]): unknown { // Extract key results and state for continue-as-new const activityResults: Record = {}; for (const event of events) { if (event.type === "activity_completed") { const { idempotencyKey, result } = event.payload as { idempotencyKey: string; result: unknown; }; activityResults[idempotencyKey] = result; } } return { activityResults, eventCount: events.length }; } private async handleFailure( workflowId: WorkflowId, runId: RunId, dag: DagDefinition, error: unknown ): Promise { // Execute compensations in reverse order const state = await kv.get(["workflows", workflowId, "state"]); if (!state.value) return; const retryState = state.value.retryState; const policy = dag.nodes.values().next().value?.retryPolicy; if (policy && retryState.attempt < policy.maxAttempts) { // Retry with exponential backoff const delay = Math.min( policy.initialInterval * Math.pow(policy.backoffCoefficient, retryState.attempt), policy.maxInterval ); await kv.set(["workflows", workflowId, "state"], { ...state.value, retryState: { attempt: retryState.attempt + 1, lastFailure: String(error), nextRetryAt: Date.now() + delay, }, }); // Schedule retry via queue await kv.enqueue( { type: "retry", workflowId, runId }, { delay } ); } else { // Execute compensations await this.executeCompensations(workflowId, runId, dag); await kv.set(["workflows", workflowId, "state"], { ...state.value, status: "failed", completedAt: Date.now(), }); } } private async executeCompensations( workflowId: WorkflowId, runId: RunId, dag: DagDefinition ): Promise { const events: WorkflowEvent[] = []; for await (const event of kv.list({ prefix: ["workflows", workflowId, "run", runId, "events"], })) { events.push(event.value); } // Find completed activities and run their compensations in reverse const completedActivities = events .filter(e => e.type === "activity_completed") .reverse(); for (const event of completedActivities) { const activityId = event.activityId; if (!activityId) continue; const node = [...dag.nodes.values()].find(n => n.activity === activityId); if (node?.compensation) { try { const primitive = this.registry.get(node.compensation); if (primitive) { const ctx = await this.security.createContext(workflowId, runId, primitive); await this.runtime.executeActivity(primitive, ctx, event.payload); } } catch (compError) { // Log compensation failure but continue console.error(`Compensation failed for ${activityId}:`, compError); } } } } } // Entry point const harness = new WorkflowHarnessImpl(); // Start queue listeners harness["timerManager"]. listenForTimers(async (msg) => { console. log("Timer fired:", msg); // Handle timer in workflow }); // Start workflow execution const runId = await harness. start("my-workflow-1", dagDefinition, { input: "data" }); console. log("Started workflow:", runId); Workflow Definition (DAG + Temporal Config) // examples/workflow_definition. ts import { WorkflowHarnessImpl } from "../harness/entry. ts";
